{"parentUuid":"e9299a11-d05c-42ef-a4fa-1f0f6fce659d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"539f7063-fdc4-4ff5-83ce-65ee18688c37","agentId":"a6a165a11de3f31fe","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01K9QyxewGCUCW2AndM74vbj","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: index\n     3→title: \"Mardov Campaign — Chapter Index\"\n     4→---\n     5→\n     6→# Mardov Campaign — Chapter Index\n     7→\n     8→> 102+ chapters across 4 acts. The campaign of [[Sabine Ra]]'s awakened champions against [[Mordred]], [[Vecna]], and the forces seeking to collapse the barrier between Mardov and V-Mardov.\n     9→\n    10→## Acts\n    11→\n    12→### [[act-1/index|Act 1 — The Awakening]] (Chapters 0-15)\n    13→The party is recruited, betrayed, killed, reborn with [[Sabine Ra]]'s soul fragments, and liberates [[Argos]]. Three prophecies are delivered, establishing the four Zeniths and Anchors.\n    14→\n    15→### [[act-2/index|Act 2 — The Voyage and the Underdark]] (Chapters 17-41)\n    16→Sea voyage to the [[Magister's Circle]], discovery of the Zodiac system, descent into the Underdark to face [[Peraxis Phyrexia]], and the [[Forgesworn]] legacy revealed.\n    17→\n    18→### [[act-3/index|Act 3 — The Zenith Wars]] (Chapters 42-87 + Reunion)\n    19→The campaign splits into Dwarves and Tieflings (later \"The Distillery\") for 45+ chapters of parallel play. Strongholds built, followers gained, individual character arcs deepened.\n    20→\n    21→### [[act-4/index|Act 4 — The Final War]] (Chapters 88-102 + Reunion)\n    22→Craftkeepers and Distillery tracks continue. The Good Guys Guild forms. [[Gard'Daemon]] approaches.\n    23→\n    24→## The Three Prophecies\n    25→\n    26→> [!quote] First Prophecy (Ruth, Chapter 9)\n    27→> \"THE ANCHORS FALL, THE REALM ASUNDER / HEARTS OF STONE GATHERED FROM PLUNDER / DEATH INCARNATE KNOCKS AT THE DOOR / BELOW THE BLOOD STAR BEGINS THE FINAL WAR!\"\n    28→\n    29→> [!quote] Second Prophecy (Hag Coven, Chapter 12)\n    30→> \"DOUBLE BUBBLE TOIL AND TROUBLE / GARD'DAEMON IS NIGH, THE FIRST ZENITH IS RUBBLE...\"\n    31→\n    32→> [!quote] Third Prophecy (MIA, Chapter 13)\n    33→> \"As the rain falls on the fallow harvest... the anchor of famine shall be lifted...\"\n    34→\n    35→## The Four Zeniths\n    36→\n    37→| Zenith | Gemstone | Anchor | Horseman | Champion |\n    38→|--------|----------|--------|----------|----------|\n    39→| [[Forest's Zenith]] | Emerald | [[Anchor of Death]] | Death | [[Brigitte]] |\n    40→| [[Mountain's Zenith]] | Garnet | [[Anchor of War]] | War | [[Bergs]] |\n    41→| [[Winter's Zenith]] | Sapphire | [[Anchor of Pestilence]] | Pestilence | [[Stalwart Little]] |\n    42→| [[Harvest's Zenith]] | Ruby | [[Anchor of Famine]] | Famine | [[Nyx]] |\n    43→\n    44→## Data Completeness\n    45→\n    46→| Act | Chapters with Story Text | Chapters Pending |\n    47→|-----|-------------------------|-----------------|\n    48→| Act 1 | 14 of 15 | 1 (Ch15 recap) |\n    49→| Act 2 | 18 of 25 | 7 (Ch16, 38-41 empty/minimal) |\n    50→| Act 3 | ~10 with detail | ~36 pending (Google Docs) |\n    51→| Act 4 | ~4 with detail | ~12 pending (Google Docs) |\n    52→"}]},"uuid":"4b61ea2e-ea27-4b74-902f-a3b03bca6ff9","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:10:55.347Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e9299a11-d05c-42ef-a4fa-1f0f6fce659d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"7c0c50ca-242a-4592-9d2d-55e563fb3625","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"539f7063-fdc4-4ff5-83ce-65ee18688c37","agentId":"a6a165a11de3f31fe","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016gi4EZBGf8x92FipLJytwL","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: chapter\n     3→act: 5\n     4→number: 104\n     5→title: \"Chapter 104\"\n     6→pcs_present: []\n     7→locations: []\n     8→npcs_introduced: []\n     9→items_found: []\n    10→source: [\"NFS/Chapters\"]\n    11→has_recording: false\n    12→has_slideshow: false\n    13→adaptation_priority: \"\"\n    14→key_moments: []\n    15→visual_beats: []\n    16→dialogue_highlights: []\n    17→---\n    18→\n    19→## DM Lead-In\n    20→\n    21→### Slide 1\n    22→\n    23→Chapter 104\n    24→\n    25→---\n    26→\n    27→### Slide 2\n    28→\n    29→MAP\n    30→\n    31→“Well, well, well finally. I have all three of you here. Welcome, to the First Forge”\n    32→\n    33→~ Reginald Providence\n    34→\n    35→---\n    36→\n    37→### Slide 3\n    38→\n    39→Last We Saw Our Heroes…\n    40→\n    41→---\n    42→\n    43→### Slide 4\n    44→\n    45→Goals Cheat Sheet\n    46→\n    47→Main Goal - Prevent Guard’Daemon\n    48→\n    49→Mountain’s Zenith: Beneath Vag Mahar\u000bAnchor of War: Daina’s Axe (unascended)\u000bHeart of the Forge: Fused with Axe\u000bGod: Therzadun (Sealed behind Iron Doors)\n    50→\n    51→Forest’s Zenith: Above Machu\u000bAnchor of Death: Brigit’s Locket\u000bHeart of the Wilde: Fused with Finkle Hollingsworth/Vecna\u000bGod: Vecna (Avatar Awakened)\n    52→\n    53→Winter’s Zenith: Bynar / Halls of Winter\u000bAnchor of Pestilence: Horn of Winter?\u000bHeart of the Faith: Fen Darkeyes took it with him\u000bGod: Tiamat (Avatar Awakened)\n    54→\n    55→Harvest’s Zenith: Autumn Feywilde in ruins of old Uma\u000bAnchor of Famine: Diadem last seen in the Halls of Reflection\u000bHeart of the Progenitors: In the hands of Grazz’t Demon Prince of Pleasure\u000bGod: Asmodeus (Sealed with Grazz’t in possession of 10/13 seals, missing only 2 Angel Seals)\n    56→\n    57→---\n    58→\n    59→### Slide 5\n    60→\n    61→The Smaller Picture\n    62→\n    63→The Vag Mahar is occupied territory by the Mindflayers beholden to Therzadun. Their leader, “Ghost Eye” Thorin, nowhere to be found\n    64→\n    65→The party is currently trying to ascend the Anchor of War within the First Forge, however there was a trap, half the party now must fight Vellem the Reforged and the other half must fight Reginald Providence, Lich of Therzadun.\n    66→\n    67→The party arrived with the Craftkeeper Rebels who agreed to divert the majority of the cities defenses to a mine known to serve as a well for Drab\n    68→\n    69→---\n    70→\n    71→### Slide 6\n    72→\n    73→The Mountain is Coming!\n    74→\n    75→The party spent the first half of their day warning nearby cities and townships of the impending doom presented by Goldemdrum the Elemental Evil of Earth’s migration to the fated battlefield with Fire, Wind and Water. Unlike Wind and Fire, who are already there with minimal collateral damage, Golemdrum seems content to cause as much damage as possible on his way.\n    76→\n    77→---\n    78→\n    79→### Slide 7\n    80→\n    81→Hearing the Voices of Monethern\n    82→\n    83→One stop on their way was Monethern, the birthplace of their friend Fen Darkeyes, whom they had hoped to find while warning the city. Instead, they met with a number of the Voices of Veritasium Palace, the most sympathetic of which was Trevor the Voice of Heart. Together, they agreed to send help to the party’s assault on the Vag Mahar and evacuate everyone they could!\n    84→\n    85→Trevor Cordin - Heart\n    86→\n    87→Coralinus Tovensmore - Truth\n    88→\n    89→Lumeire Huntsman - Paupers\n    90→\n    91→---\n    92→\n    93→### Slide 8\n    94→\n    95→Beaten, Bloody & Nearly Alone\n    96→\n    97→Bergs “Barnabus” Fireforge survived his parties delve into the White Hills of St. Brutus purely on a technicality. His mushroom reanimated body firmly planting itself into the ground with every step, he made his way to the Vag Mahar after being drawn to the hundreds of voices of the Drab within. He carried with him the only other surviving member of his party Kelemtor, though merely the risen’s head remained. Through, luck or fate, he was reunited with his sister Daina and her friends just in time to meet Theosodia and the Craftkeepers for his third attempt at freeing the Dwarven Capital.\n    98→\n    99→His every colored beard now bleached white, unable to control the flow of myconid information from Birtcoin. Our Craftmaster is in a bad way.\n   100→\n   101→---\n   102→\n   103→### Slide 9\n   104→\n   105→Beefed up Main Gate\n   106→\n   107→Repaired/Reinforced Upper Tier Gate\n   108→\n   109→Trashed Docks\n   110→\n   111→Crowded with Drab\n   112→\n   113→Broken Timekeeping Device & old tunnel\n   114→\n   115→Collapsed Tunnel Leading Up\n   116→\n   117→The Military Strategy\n   118→\n   119→The Craftkeepers head to the Blackbeard Lower Ward to the MindFlayer Mine to create a distraction and see if assaulting the Mindflayer hive beneath.\n   120→\n   121→The Party would sneak through the Blackbeard’s Middle Ward to Upper Ward and over to the Stone Sanctum in the Brownbeard’s Upper Ward, passing through the Redbeard’s Upper Ward on the way.\n   122→\n   123→---\n   124→\n   125→### Slide 10\n   126→\n   127→The Troubling Politics of Drow\n   128→\n   129→While sneaking their way through the city, it became clear that there was a tiered system for citizens with Drow in a quasi-foreign dignitary status. Once at the Blackbeard’s Upper Ward, aka Elysium, Nocturnas Wayne was approached by Veyrea Hysteil, leader of a faction of Drow who believe that the Darkest Night prophecy is still possible. The party avoids a fight by declaring fealty to the Wayne Family. They agree to meet later, if there is one.\n   130→\n   131→Note that pre-vamipire/Shadar Kai Nocturnus has a wanted poster up. All Drow except 3 are now Vampires, meaning one of the 3 must be the one to set the Darkest Night.\n   132→\n   133→---\n   134→\n   135→### Slide 11\n   136→\n   137→Encounter with Plaguebringer\n   138→\n   139→At the ruins of the Dragon’s Carapace Workshop, the party encounters Pyraxis Phyrexia, Vampiric Illithid Lich of Vecna and creator of the Drab Spellplague, who gives them a warning about the two-eyed Beholder Vellem the Reforged, so that they can be better equipped to defeat his rival, Reginald Providence. The runin was tense, however the disgraced Mindflayer left before things could get too violent.\n   140→\n   141→His warning was that if both eyes look upon you, you will be transported to join Reginald within the Stone Sanctum, which would be a trap.\n   142→\n   143→---\n   144→\n   145→### Slide 12\n   146→\n   147→Traps Triggered, Let’s Split Up Gang!\n   148→\n   149→Inside the Stone Sanctum:\n   150→\n   151→Daina “Diesa” Fireforge\n   152→\n   153→Bergs “Barnabus” Fireforge\n   154→\n   155→Nocturnas Wayne\n   156→\n   157→Reginald Providence\n   158→\n   159→a mysterious huddled henchman\n   160→\n   161→Outside the Stone Sanctum:\n   162→\n   163→Varis Laidon\n   164→\n   165→Sprocket Overspark\n   166→\n   167→Brigit Wildfire\n   168→\n   169→HAL\n   170→\n   171→Vellem The Reforged\n   172→\n   173→\n   174→## Forgesworn Maghar\n   175→\n   176→### Slide 1\n   177→\n   178→Forgesworn Maghar\n   179→\n   180→---\n   181→\n   182→### Slide 2\n   183→\n   184→Forgesworn Maghar\n   185→\n   186→Moradin’s Coat of Arms:\n   187→\n   188→Each has a transferable tattoo passed from Maghar -> Apprentice\n   189→\n   190→Gifted to the Dwarves thanks to Maghar Greyspear, Paragon of Muradin\n   191→\n   192→Combined effort by Laudgar Duergar to create Warforged out of all 13 Maghar to fight Mindflayers\n   193→\n   194→13 of which must have died to open the Mountain’s Zenith\n   195→\n   196→Bronzebeards Creed\n   197→\n   198→\"In the thrill of creation, there is one dwarven kin,\n   199→\n   200→I shall guard the secrets of my art as closely as I study them\n   201→\n   202→Upon Muradin's Honor, we shall guard the realm with his gift\n   203→\n   204→The First Forge bind them, Laudgar's price we gladly give\"\n   205→\n   206→---\n   207→\n   208→### Slide 3\n   209→\n   210→Meet The Maghar: Helm\n   211→\n   212→Bless Artifact: Helm.\n   213→\n   214→Jakob Hardhammer blesses the helm of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed helm grants the wearer +2 to spell saves while within range. Only one Helm may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   215→\n   216→---\n   217→\n   218→### Slide 4\n   219→\n   220→Meet the Maghar: Breastplate\n   221→\n   222→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Breastplate.\n   223→\n   224→Bartrim Hadrin blesses the Breastplate of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Breastplate grants the wearer +2 to AC while within range. Only one Breastplate may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   225→\n   226→---\n   227→\n   228→### Slide 5\n   229→\n   230→Meet the Maghar: Pauldrons\n   231→\n   232→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Pauldrons.\n   233→\n   234→Gareth Gorunn blesses the Boots of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Pauldrons grants the wearer the ability to lift twice their normal amount and advantage on Strength Checks while within range. Only one Set of Pauldrons may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   235→\n   236→---\n   237→\n   238→### Slide 6\n   239→\n   240→Meet the Maghar: Gauntlets\n   241→\n   242→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Gauntlets.\n   243→\n   244→Gavin Haldrin blesses the Gauntlets of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Gauntlets grants the wearer +2 to hit on attacks from within range. Only one Gauntlets may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   245→\n   246→---\n   247→\n   248→### Slide 7\n   249→\n   250→Meet the Maghar: Greaves\n   251→\n   252→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Greaves.\n   253→\n   254→Hammond Jack blesses the Greaves of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Greaves grants the wearer a swim speed within magma and immunity to fire damage while within range. Only one Pair of Greaves may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   255→\n   256→---\n   257→\n   258→### Slide 8\n   259→\n   260→Meet the Maghar: Boots\n   261→\n   262→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Boots.\n   263→\n   264→Bardin Caveskin blesses the Boots of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Boots grants the wearer +10ft of movement while within range. Only one Pair of Boots may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   265→\n   266→---\n   267→\n   268→### Slide 9\n   269→\n   270→Meet the Maghar: Clockwork\n   271→\n   272→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Clockwork.\n   273→\n   274→Decan Smithrunner blesses the Belt of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Belt grants the wearer +2/-2 to any roll once per turn while within range. Only one Clockwork may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   275→\n   276→---\n   277→\n   278→### Slide 10\n   279→\n   280→Meet the Maghar: Belt\n   281→\n   282→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Belt.\n   283→\n   284→Reginald Providence blesses the Belt of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Belt grants the wearer the ability to hold two sets of concentration while within range. By passing ownership of a spell currently being concentrated on while touching the statue. Only one Belt may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   285→\n   286→---\n   287→\n   288→### Slide 11\n   289→\n   290→Meet the Maghar: Sword\n   291→\n   292→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Sword.\n   293→\n   294→Slyvia Hadrin blesses the Sword of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The Blessed Sword grants the wearer +2 to damage on attacks from within range. Only one Sword may be blessed at a time. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   295→\n   296→---\n   297→\n   298→### Slide 12\n   299→\n   300→Meet the Maghar: Shield\n   301→\n   302→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Shield.\n   303→\n   304→Pickle Rockgut blesses the Shield of a target within a 20ft of his statue. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   315→\n   316→---\n   317→\n   318→### Slide 14\n   319→\n   320→Meet the Maghar: Hammer\n   321→\n   322→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Hammer.\n   323→\n   324→Hiln Blunderbeat blesses the Hammer of a target within a 20ft of his statue. The full blessing may only be granted once per week.\n   325→\n   326→---\n   327→\n   328→### Slide 15\n   329→\n   330→Meet the Maghar: Spear\n   331→\n   332→Channel Divinity - Bless Artifact: Spear.\n   333→\n   334→Torvald Greyspear blesses the Spear of a target within a 20ft of his statue. If the target is also under the effects of the other blessings of Muradin, these effects last until the end of the next long rest. []\n    17→---\n    18→\n    19→## DM Lead-In\n    20→\n    21→<!-- PDF extraction requires pdftotext or pandoc: DM Lead In.pdf -->\n    22→\n    23→\n    24→## Plan\n    25→\n    26→Tiamat fight ending.\n    27→\n    28→Need a vignette detailing what Nocturnus Wayne will do next (return to underdark to restart the Drow population)\n    29→\n    30→- Killing Tiamat/Ashara will allow Honor to take its final form: her name will be **Promise Wayne**\n    31→\n    32→Upon killing Tiamat, Vecna will have gained the knowledge from both fallen Gods, which also means that he will have the knowledge of how to bring about Oblivion and to Subjugate even the most powerful of beings.\n    33→\n    34→- He will use this to call the Dragons to Machu\n    35→\n    36→The Ash falling on Mardov The directory hierarchy itself encodes the geographic relationships.*\n    10→\n    11→---\n    12→\n    13→## Top-Level Regions\n    14→\n    15→```\n    16→Places/\n    17→├── Bynar              (arctic/mountain region — Winter's Zenith)\n    18→├── DawnHaven           (largest region — Forest's & Harvest's Zeniths)\n    19→├── Islands             (archipelago, Qual'Xar, sea routes)\n    20→├── Monethern           (faith/dwarven region — Mountain's Zenith)\n    21→├── Other               (miscellaneous battle maps)\n    22→├── Outer Planes        (extraplanar locations)\n    23→├── The Moon            (lunar location)\n    24→├── Underdark           (subterranean realm)\n    25→└── Vartos              (magical/academic region — Valley of Anchors)\n    26→```\n    27→\n    28→**Top-level map images (not in folders):**\n    29→- `Guard'Daemon.jpg` — The apocalyptic event\n    30→- `MARDOV.jpg` — Continental map\n    31→- `MARDOV (Post Mages Tournament).jpg` — Map after a major event\n    32→- `Mardov -- post cloud city incident.jpg` — Map after Cloud City falls\n    33→- `Mollys Revenge.jpg` — Ship or location\n    34→- `Vag Mahar Mountain View.jpg` — Dwarven city vista\n    35→- `Vision of the Fourth Guard'Daemon.png` — Prophetic vision\n    36→\n    37→---\n    38→\n    39→## Bynar (Winter's Zenith Region)\n    40→\n    41→```\n    42→Bynar/\n    43→└── Cradle/\n    44→    ├── Mt. Bynar\n    45→    ├── Mt. Dragonspine/\n    46→    │   └── FrostFang Village\n    47→    └── Shard Mountain\n    48→```\n    49→\n    50→**Lore Notes:** Home to the dragon-kin tribe known as the Winter's Curators (now ruined). The Winter's Zenith is within the mountain of Bynar, guarded by a White Dragon. Connected to the Anchor of Pestilence.\n    51→\n    52→---\n    53→\n    54→## DawnHaven (Primary Region)\n    55→\n    56→The most extensively mapped region, containing multiple sub-regions and hundreds of locations.\n    57→\n    58→```\n    59→DawnHaven/\n    60→├── Argos\n    61→├── Cloud City_Storm Peaks (slash conflict)/\n    62→│   ├── 5 Cities of Giants/\n    63→│   │   ├── Cloudheim\n    64→│   │   ├── Fireheim\n    65→│   │   ├── Frostheim/\n    66→│   │   │   └── Absent Frost Giants\n    67→│   │   ├── Hillheim/\n    68→│   │   │   └── Hallan Opera Hall\n    69→│   │ {"parentUuid":"6ca906e3-f346-4ff2-95f3-56e647db98d9","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"a6a165a11de3f31fe","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CppuYVyYFh8gBR5ZMikDLT","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"visual-moments-catalog\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/visual-moments-catalog.md\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→# Visual Moments Catalog -- Mardov Campaign\n     8→\n     9→Art director's sourcebook. 100 most visually striking scenes from Brigit's player notes, ranked by visual potential. Sourced from Chapters 28-136.\n    10→\n    11→---\n    12→\n    13→## Tier 1: Iconic Campaign-Defining Images (1-20)\n    14→\n    15→### 1. The Locket Ascends -- All Thirteen Zodiac Souls Unite (Chapter 120)\n    16→**Scene**: Brigit holds the locket aloft as the final progenitor soul enters it. The locket ascends to full power, radiating light that turns every undead creature in her presence. The twelve zodiac forms swirl within the locket's inner realm -- wolf, stag, doe, fox, pangolin, serpent, rabbit, and more -- orbiting four elemental cores.\n    17→**Visual**: Central figure (Brigit) arms raised, golden locket blazing with twelve distinct animal silhouettes spiraling outward in concentric rings. Undead creatures recoiling and disintegrating in waves from the light. Cathedral-scale radiance.\n    18→**Characters**: Brigit (center), zodiac spirits (ethereal)\n    19→**Pilot relevance**: No\n    20→**ComfyUI prompt sketch**: `fantasy woman barbarian holding glowing golden locket above head, twelve spectral animal spirits spiraling outward, undead creatures dissolving in holy light, epic divine power moment, dark battlefield background, volumetric god rays, golden and white light against darkness`\n    21→\n    22→### 2. Daina Beheads the Undead Dragon with the Anchor of War (Chapter 98)\n    23→**Scene**: After a brutal fight against the undead dragon Silvanesh, Daina delivers the killing blow. She shield-slams, then with a devastating swing of the Anchor of War axe, slices off the dragon's head. The emaciated corpse reveals the dragon died long ago -- it was a puppet all along.\n    24→**Visual**: Low angle shot. Daina mid-swing, axe trailing war-rune light. Dragon's severed head separating, revealing an emaciated, hollow body beneath. The puppet strings of necromantic energy dissipating upward.\n    25→**Characters**: Daina, Silvanesh (undead green dragon), Ashara in background\n    26→**Pilot relevance**: No\n    27→**ComfyUI prompt sketch**: `dwarven woman warrior mid-swing with massive glowing runic battleaxe, severing head of undead green dragon, dragon body emaciated and hollow, dark magical puppet strings dissolving upward, dynamic action pose, low angle dramatic shot, dark forest battlefield`\n    28→\n    29→### 3. Brigit's Ritual of Reincarnation at Dawn (Chapter 87)\n    30→**Scene**: At dawn in Faughart, a procession of two lines of elves approaches. Brigit, wearing flower crowns, covers her hands in ceremonial oil and ointment, reaches through the grave dirt, and casts the reincarnation spell. Gnolls howl and dig through the dirt. The children of the forest place their objects into the same dirt. Bodies are reborn as entirely different races.\n    31→**Visual**: Golden dawn light filtering through forest canopy. Brigit kneeling at a flower-covered grave, arms plunged into the earth, oil-gleaming hands glowing with green druidic magic. Two lines of elves flanking in ceremonial formation. Gnolls digging reverently. Fern and wildflower erupting from the grave.\n    32→**Characters**: Brigit (kneeling), elves (procession), children of the forest, gnolls\n    33→**Pilot relevance**: No\n    34→**ComfyUI prompt sketch**: `druid woman kneeling at flower-covered grave at dawn, hands thrust into glowing earth, green magical energy, two lines of elves in ceremonial robes flanking path, forest clearing, golden morning light, flower crowns, ceremonial rebirth ritual, reverent atmosphere`\n    35→\n    36→### 4. Sprocket's Disintegrate vs. Dallor Dallor Bill -- \"YOU'RE NOT WELCOME HERE\" (Chapter 86)\n    37→**Scene**: The Nightwalker Dallor -- a colossus taller than Sprocket's tower -- looms over the distillery beach. Sprocket flies forward on his broom, screams \"YOU'RE NOT WELCOME HERE\" and casts Disintegrate, punching a hole clean through the creature. Zombies immediately pour from the wound.\n    38→**Visual**: Tiny gnome on a broomstick against a kaiju-scale nightwalker silhouette. A beam of green disintegration magic lancing through the creature's torso. The hole fills with swarming zombie forms. Beach siege warfare raging below.\n    39→**Characters**: Sprocket (on broom), Dallor (nightwalker), armies below\n    40→**Pilot relevance**: No\n    41→**ComfyUI prompt sketch**: `tiny gnome wizard on broomstick firing green disintegration beam at colossal shadow nightwalker monster on beach, hole in monster filling with zombies, siege warfare below, massive scale difference, dramatic fantasy battle, stormy sky`\n    42→\n    43→### 5. Nocturnous Drinks Dragon Blood and Ascends the Tower (Chapter 120)\n    44→**Scene**: In the Halls of Reflection, during the fight against the ancient black dragon Xax'thalis, Nocturnous -- the vampiric monk -- drinks the dragon's blood mid-combat. He fires a crossbow rope upward, ascends the tower in a blur, and unleashes a flurry of blows on the paralyzed dragon clinging to the frozen tower.\n    45→**Visual**: Vertical composition. Icy tower stretching upward. Dark-skinned drow monk ascending a rope like a needle, blood dripping from his mouth. A massive paralyzed black dragon clinging to the tower above. Abishai demons circling. Winter storm raging.\n    46→**Characters**: Nocturnous, Xax'thalis (ancient black dragon), abishai\n    47→**Pilot relevance**: No\n    48→**ComfyUI prompt sketch**: `dark elf monk ascending rope on frozen tower, blood dripping from mouth, massive paralyzed black dragon clinging to tower above, demon creatures circling, winter storm, vertical dramatic composition, ice and darkness, gothic fantasy`\n    49→\n    50→### 6. Brigit Turns into a Fire Elemental and Escapes the Temple (Chapter 90)\n    51→**Scene**: Trapped in the House of Life and Death, captured and chained as a dinosaur, Brigit drops the form, turns into a fire elemental, and bursts free. The ancient Machu temple erupts in flame as she and her companions flee from the Priestess of Pain's guards.\n    52→**Visual**: A figure of living flame bursting from stone chains in an ancient temple. Hieroglyphics on the walls illuminated by the fire. Guards recoiling. Stone pillars casting dramatic shadows.\n    53→**Characters**: Brigit (fire elemental), Cersei, HAL, temple guards\n    54→**Pilot relevance**: No\n    55→**ComfyUI prompt sketch**: `humanoid figure made entirely of fire bursting free from stone chains in ancient temple, hieroglyphic walls illuminated by firelight, guards recoiling, ancient stone pillars, dramatic shadows, magical escape scene`\n    56→\n    57→### 7. The Hero's Feast Before the Beholder Battle (Chapter 78)\n    58→**Scene**: At dawn in the Valley of Anchors, Brigit prepares a barbarian hero's feast beneath the great tree. A primitive charcuterie spread on the ground, a campfire with smoldering rosemary and sage, war paint made from mud and berries. Brigit paints beholder eyes all over Erlax the actual beholder. The party dons ceremonial bells, bones, and herbs.\n    59→**Visual**: Golden morning light. A circle of warriors seated around a primitive feast with a campfire center. A massive beholder floating nearby, covered in painted eyes. Characters painting each other's faces and arms with henna-like patterns. {"parentUuid":"6e975313-abb0-4d0b-ad91-82e0cd4aa757","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"a6a165a11de3f31fe","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01D6UEnaU1yu1PhDkHyBat7c","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"pilot-story-bible\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/pilot-story-bible.md\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→# Mardov Pilot A -- Story Bible\n     8→\n     9→**Series**: Heroes of Mardov\n    10→**Format**: Gothic anime motion webtoon (vertical scroll)\n    11→**Pilot Length**: 80 panels across 7 acts\n    12→**Visual Style**: Dark fantasy, manhwa ink lines, Illustrious XL 2.0 + gothic LoRA stack\n    13→**Aspect Ratio**: 832x1216 (2:3 webtoon tall)\n    14→\n    15→---\n    16→\n    17→## Logline\n    18→\n    19→When a band of strangers answers a military summons to a besieged port town, they are led into an ancient dungeon beneath the streets -- and into a betrayal that reveals a sealed door bearing the crest of the Raven Queen, a god who should not have business with the living.\n    20→\n    21→---\n    22→\n    23→## Synopsis\n    24→\n    25→On a windswept coastal cliff, Conquis Abbey receives an urgent military summons. Riders depart at dawn, traveling through a darkening landscape scarred by something predatory, and arrive at a port town bracing for a threat it cannot name. Within the town, a secret forge produces a weapon that burns with unnatural light -- a god-killer blade whose very existence hints at divine stakes the townspeople cannot comprehend.\n    26→\n    27→As dusk settles, we meet the adventurers who will form our party: Sprocket, a gnome diviner reading fate in his cramped carriage study; Brigit, a druid who shifts from wolf to woman at the town walls; Stalwart, a golden-scaled dragonborn paladin who commands attention on every street; and Nyx, a tiefling warlock scribbling secrets in a candlelit inn room. Three more figures flash past in quick cuts -- a wild-eyed mage losing control of her own magic, a furiously short man elbowing through crowds, and a gnome with clever hands and too many pockets. All roads lead to the same recruitment yard.\n    28→\n    29→There, a tyrannical gnome bureaucrat named Finkle -- three feet of immaculate suit and pure disdain -- takes command of the recruits with a triangle diagram placing himself firmly at the top. The awkward strangers spar, disperse, and reconvene at a tavern, where fragile camaraderie is shattered by the arrival of Jorgen, a bleeding ranger who crashes through the door with kobolds on his heels. After a vicious tavern brawl, a cryptic note draws the party underground through hidden sewer passages into architecture older than civilization itself. They face a kobold standoff in the dark, navigate trapped corridors, and survive a descending ceiling carved with a single word: KNEEL.\n    30→\n    31→At the bottom of a spiral staircase, crimson light replaces torchlight. They enter a cathedral-scale ritual chamber dominated by four supernatural waterfalls -- blood-red, acid-green, mercury-orange, and soul-blue -- cascading into stone basins around a central altar. This is the pilot's full-page revelation: a space that should not exist beneath a port town, radiating power that predates human memory. Then Jorgen, the ranger who led them here, turns his weapon on the party. In the chaos that follows, they discover a massive sealed steel door bearing the crest of the Raven Queen -- concentric rings of forged metal inscribed in a language older than the common tongue. They stand before it, battered, betrayed, and confronted by a question none of them came here to answer: what lies behind the door, and why does a death goddess need it sealed?\n    32→\n    33→---\n    34→\n    35→## Act Structure\n    36→\n    37→### Act 1: The Call (Panels 1-7)\n    38→\n    39→**Setting**: Conquis Abbey, a crumbling gothic monastery on a coastal cliff.\n    40→**Tone**: Ancient weight, institutional weariness, gathering dread.\n    41→**Color Temperature**: Cool greys, storm-light, candlelit amber interiors.\n    42→\n    43→The pilot opens with absolute silence and scale. Conquis Abbey is introduced as a place older than the conflict it now serves -- beautiful and broken, its flying buttresses cracked by salt wind, its gargoyles eroded into ghosts of their original forms. Ravens circle the bell tower. The sea churns below. This is not a place of adventure. This is a place that has seen too much.\n    44→\n    45→Inside, the great hall has been repurposed as a war room. Maps and military correspondence cover every surface. Half-eaten meals suggest people have not left this room in days. A rain-soaked messenger arrives bearing a sealed scroll -- the wax seal is military, official, the kind that means mobilization. An officer reads the summons aloud to grim faces. The camera moves to the war table: a top-down map view with fresh red markers showing threat positions, and a single circled destination -- the port town.\n    46→\n    47→Night falls. The abbey goes dark except for one amber window where someone is still deciding. This is the last quiet moment before movement begins.\n    48→\n    49→**Panel Breakdown**:\n    50→\n    51→| Panel | Title | Description | Characters | Mood | Camera |\n    52→|-------|-------|-------------|------------|------|--------|\n    53→| 1 | Conquis Abbey | Wide establishing shot. Ancient gothic abbey on a coastal cliff above grey churning sea. Crumbling buttresses, faintly glowing stained glass, circling ravens, ocean mist. No humans visible. | None | Ancient weight, isolation | Wide establishing, slightly low angle |\n    54→| 2 | Abbey Interior -- War Room | Interior of the great hall. Vaulted stone ceiling, long oak table covered in maps and correspondence, candelabras with melting candles, military banners in scarlet and gold, dusty stained-glass light. | None (implied figures) | Institutional tension | Mid-wide interior, eye level |\n    55→| 3 | The Summons | A rain-soaked messenger kneels at the threshold, mud-covered, cloak torn from hard riding. He holds out a sealed scroll with a prominent wax seal. | Messenger | Urgency, exhaustion | Mid-shot, slightly above |\n    56→| 4 | The Seal | Extreme close-up of the red wax seal on the scroll. Official military crest -- mobilization, not correspondence. Travel-worn parchment, water stains. | None (object focus) | Dread, authority | Extreme close-up, macro |\n    57→| 5 | The Order | An officer reads the scroll aloud. Grim, controlled expression. The assembled room listens with faces that have heard too much bad news to react visibly. | Officer, war room assembly | Controlled despair | Mid-shot, eye level |\n    58→| 6 | The Map | Top-down view of the war table. Aged fantasy map of coastal region, faded ink roads and town symbols. Fresh red markers showing threats, force positions, and a single circled destination. | None (map focus) | Strategic clarity | Overhead/bird's eye |\n    59→| 7 | Abbey Night -- Decision | Exterior, night. The abbey is mostly dark but one window glows amber. Moonlight on ocean waves below. A solitary figure visible as a shadow in the lit window. | Solitary figure (silhouette) | Solitude, resolve | Wide exterior, mid-distance |\n    60→\n    61→---\n    62→\n    63→### Act 2: The Journey (Panels 8-14)\n    64→\n    65→**Setting**: The road from Conquis Abbey to the port town.\n    66→**Tone**: Cinematic travel, environmental storytelling, escalating unease.\n    67→**Color Temperature**: Pale dawn transitioning to dark forest greens and greys, ending in overcast harbor light.\n    68→\n    69→Dawn breaks. Riders in scarlet cloaks depart through the abbey gates, silhouetted against pale sunrise. The road sequence is a landscape poem: winding through dark autumn forests, across old stone bridges over misty streams. The world is beautiful and wounded. An abandoned merchant cart with deep claw marks -- no bodies, which is worse than bodies -- tells the audience everything about what waits ahead without a word of dialogue.\n    70→\n    71→The port town appears from a hillside: stone buildings around a natural harbor, smoke from chimneys, a handful of ships at anchor. It looks safe from a distance. At the gate, a queue of merchants and refugees tells a different story. Inside, citizens board windows, carry water barrels, and drill in courtyards. A notice board covered in recruitment posters and missing persons reports is the first direct storytelling panel -- the audience can read the town's fear in its own words.\n    72→\n    73→**Panel Breakdown**:\n    74→\n    75→| Panel | Title | Description | Characters | Mood | Camera |\n    76→|-------|-------|-------------|------------|------|--------|\n    77→| 8 | Dawn Riders Depart | Dawn. Riders on horseback through abbey gates, silhouetted against pale sunrise. Scarlet cloaks streaming, horses' breath visible in cold air. Dusty road ahead through dark landscape. | Riders (silhouette) | Purpose, departure | Wide, backlit |\n    78→| 9 | The Road | Wide landscape. Riders small on a winding road through dark forest. Autumn leaves drifting, old stone bridge, misty stream. Beautiful and foreboding. | Riders (distant) | Pastoral unease | Ultra-wide landscape |\n    79→| 10 | Roadside Discovery | Abandoned merchant cart overturned at roadside. Goods scattered, horse gone, deep claw marks in the wood. No bodies. Riders have stopped to examine it. | Riders examining | Quiet horror | Mid-wide, eye level |\n    80→| 11 | Port Town -- First Sight | Wide establishing from hillside. Port town below: stone buildings, natural harbor, wooden docks, ships at anchor, chimney smoke. Looks peaceful from distance. | None (landscape) | Deceptive calm | Wide establishing, elevated |"}]},"uuid":"2162d667-644f-4424-81f0-5aa00787b16c","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:09.727Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6e975313-abb0-4d0b-ad91-82e0cd4aa757","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"4f309369-a608-4fa7-818c-b782d80accc1","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"a6a165a11de3f31fe","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01YYSKFxsNs5iXNJopjPexUa","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"world-bible\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/world-bible.md\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→# Mardov World Bible\n     8→\n     9→*Compiled from DM source files: Ideas/, Notes/, Places/, Visions/, and the pacing study.*\n    10→\n    11→---\n    12→\n    13→## Cosmology\n    14→\n    15→### The Gods and Primal Forces\n    16→\n    17→The cosmology of Mardov revolves around a central divine conflict between twin souls: **Vecna** (god of evil secrets, animancy) and **Sabine** (goddess of life, death, and fate). They are the original twin souls, sharing a domain over death. Sabine's additional dominion over life made Vecna jealous, and his campaign to steal her powers is the cosmic engine driving the entire narrative.\n    18→\n    19→**Key Deities Referenced:**\n    20→- **Sabine Ra** — Goddess of life, death, and fate. \"Your time comes when it comes.\" Her spirit was shattered; her gifts scattered across the world. She will return as a demi-lich through the merging of Ramses and Xerxes.\n    21→- **Vecna** — God of evil secrets and animancy. His avatar rules a parallel dimension (V-MARDOV). He is \"death incarnate knocking at the door.\"\n    22→- **The Raven Queen** — Goddess of death and necromancy. Her avatar's sacrifice creates a rift between worlds.\n    23→- **Bahamut** — God of justice, worshipped at Conquis Abbey and throughout Monethern. His highest priesthood is the Silver Scales: \"All that is Just is not Righteous! All that is Righteous is not Just!\"\n    24→- **Asmodeus** — God of tyranny and mind control magic.\n    25→- **Gruumsh** — God of destruction and chaos.\n    26→- **Tiamat** — Worshipped by the vampire queen of the Underdark, who gave her soul to Tiamat for an ascension ritual.\n    27→- **Pelor** — Temple in WindHaven (Field of Pelor).\n    28→- **Avandra** — Temple in WindHaven (Avandra's Gift) and Conquis Abbey (Gardens of Avandra).\n    29→- **Ioun** — Archive of Ioun at Conquis Abbey.\n    30→- **Erathis** — Workshop of Erathis in Tinkertown, Conquis Abbey.\n    31→- **Moradin** — God of dwarves, channeled at the Corpseforge in the Underdark.\n    32→\n    33→### The Planes\n    34→\n    35→**Material Plane:** Mardov — the continent, surrounded by seas. Contains the four Zeniths.\n    36→\n    37→**Outer Planes:**\n    38→- **7 Heavens**\n    39→- **9 Hells**\n    40→- **Astral Sea** — Contains \"Betwixt\" and \"The Veil\"\n    41→- **Eaden**\n    42→- **Fae Wildes** — Four courts: Brier Patch (Spring), Flowerfield (Summer), Reaper's Bounty (Autumn), Snowcrest Sanctum (Winter). Also contains the Glittering Run.\n    43→- **ShadowFell** — Contains RavenFall, Gate of the Broken, Path of the Watchers, Shadar's Gift, Vision Gate (with rooms for individual characters: Casaval, Daina, Stalwart)\n    44→- **Vodram** — Contains Valheim and Vodram's FeyWilde (Hengefield, Serpent Meadows, Ursa Rock)\n    45→- **Uvan**\n    46→- **The Moon** — Has its own location folder, implying it's reachable/significant.\n    47→- **V-MARDOV** — The parallel dimension where Vecna's avatar rules. Mirror of the material plane. Contains a \"Death's Zenith\" that mirrors the Forest's Zenith.\n    48→\n    49→### The Four Zeniths and Keystones\n    50→\n    51→The Zeniths are ancient nexus points of cosmic power. Each contains a **Heart** (a gemstone keystone) and an **Anchor** (a weapon tied to one of the Four Horsemen). Mordred's plan is to destroy all keystones, enabling V-MORDRED's invasion.\n    52→\n    53→| Zenith | Heart | Gemstone | Anchor | Horseman | Location | Vecna's Eye |\n    54→|--------|-------|----------|--------|----------|----------|-------------|\n    55→| **Forest's Zenith** | Heart of the Wilde | Emerald | Anchor of Death (obsidian sword) | Death | Machu, DawnHaven | Green Eye |\n    56→| **Mountain's Zenith** | Heart of the Forge | Garnet | Anchor of War (hammer-axe-javelin) | War | Vag Mahar, Monethern | Red Eye |\n    57→| **Winter's Zenith** | Heart of the Faith | Sapphire | Anchor of Pestilence (pan flute / turtle skeleton) | Pestilence | Bynar | Blue Eye |\n    58→| **Harvest's Zenith** | Heart of the Progenitors | Ruby | Anchor of Famine (Diadem of Dawn) | Famine | Tygras Logath, DawnHaven | Orange Eye |\n    59→\n    60→### The Valley of Anchors"}]},"uuid":"e6101ed0-8795-41aa-b947-55fc1ea7246c","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:24.396Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4f309369-a608-4fa7-818c-b782d80accc1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"28f09d52-3178-4f54-8b4b-de1437148f7d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"a6a165a11de3f31fe","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013Nhyi9rNhS1vTKq1qbLZ7f","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: roll20-entry\n     3→title: \"From Fen\"\n     4→source: [\"Roll20 Export\"]\n     5→roll20_path: \"roll20-export/journal/000 - The Distillery/000 - Handouts/001 - From Fen/handout.json\"\n     6→---\n     7→\n     8→# From Fen\n     9→\n    10→## Notes\n    11→\n    12→Memorize this note because we will burn it when we are done reading it. I\n    13→ have cast non-detection on it to prevent prying eyes from seeing what I\n    14→ write. I visited the library in Storm Peaks today, and got a possible \n    15→lead on the location of the harvest Zenith. The last known location is \n    16→at the city of Uma, but it disappeared during some cataclysmic event \n    17→that also destroyed the city. Perhaps we can investigate there after the\n    18→ festival. The main reason I am writing this note though, is that I have\n    19→ created a plan to twart, or at least slow down Reynal that guy. During my time in the Feywild, I was entrusted the Posiden seal. I encountered Reynaldi that guy\n    20→ and he took it from me forcibly. From my understanding of Feys, he now \n    21→owes me something. If we encounter him before he breaks the seals and \n    22→opens the zenith, I believe he will be compelled to give me a gift. I \n    23→will ask that Brigitte has the opportunity to hold the Fire and Earth \n    24→seals before they are destroyed. When Brigitte has them in her \n    25→possession, we can use the Rod of Paradise to teleport to this \"Eden\" \n    26→place you guys spoke of. From there we can plan our next steps to evade Reyn that guy.\n    27→\n    28→"}]},"uuid":"ee828c55-9bc6-48dc-badb-075d0af27230","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:27.625Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"28f09d52-3178-4f54-8b4b-de1437148f7d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"3fe15d62-9346-4989-b59b-dcbe6e0b5861","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"a6a165a11de3f31fe","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AYRCejvEEwyiGuByDEMzcK","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: roll20-entry\n     3→title: \"Note Among the Research Within the Labryinth\"\n     4→source: [\"Roll20 Export\"]\n     5→roll20_path: \"roll20-export/journal/001 - The Craftmasters/000 - Handouts/000 - Note Among the Research Within the Labryinth/handout.json\"\n     6→---\n     7→\n     8→# Note Among the Research Within the Labryinth\n     9→\n    10→## Notes\n    11→\n    12→“Vespen, keep and eye on Grobollan for me. I have a mission from our new client and need to cause some terror amongst the rebels. Make sure to guide the buffoon to the location on map before our next assignment. Apparently the fool Peraxis let a bard make off with this information and I am awarded a bonus for intercepting and silencing the leak. Take care not to lose this, else I would be forced to silence you as well. To think, just because that idiot Tygras got himself killed, we are forced to be contracted out like lowly fiends. We are members of the thirteen damned! We stopped the ritual of reflection and helped recollect Emni the Undimmed for the dragons. But… at least this assignment is fun. So, fulfill your mission with the minotaur and meet me at the location on the map. If you see our next mark on the way, do us a favor and have Groballan kill him for me. Two birds with one stone…. Well in this case it’s really two beetles! As always, glory to the Lady of the Night, happy hunting.”\n    13→\n    14→"}]},"uuid":"7e8b3585-9c1c-4174-91d8-b1be4e62d7b1","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:33.421Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3fe15d62-9346-4989-b59b-dcbe6e0b5861","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"fb520fd6-db80-46e5-895d-66c86c4b1e30","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"a6a165a11de3f31fe","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XYGpkvq9xBrcDwGBTMqnke","type":"tool_result","content":"---\ntype: lore\ntitle: \"Aaura the Bard\"\nsource: [\"World Anvil\"]\nworld_anvil_category: \"\"\n---\n\n# Aaura the Bard\n\nAfter her hometown fell from the sky, this air genasi of the blue-grey Western Air Genasi set out the see the world she had never experienced. She will seek out a mentor who will quick discover her talent. Her default song is a\nmeloncholy bagpipes and harrowing voice\nShe befriended a local lamb which she has nicknamed Gretta. The two of them travel the countryside of Dawnpass searching for the Bard who she heard play a countercharm during the sacking of the Storm Peaks. Torn from her small instrument shop she snapped out of the binding the Orcish Shamans bearing the Red Handed Banner. Had she reacted a moment slower, her freedom would be lost. Her husband dead and gone. Aarua the Westwind chose to wield his sword, that even in death he would protect her.\nHer wish is to find the Pegasus Bard and hear his tale, so that she may spread the story of her people and know the purpose behind the sorrow in her life.\n===\n---\ntype: lore\ntitle: \"Volcanis the Scorched Tyrant\"\nsource: [\"World Anvil\"]\nworld_anvil_category: \"\"\n---\n\n# Volcanis the Scorched Tyrant\n\nLeader of the Red Scaled Dragons who were apart of Tiamat's Chromatic Brood. Their eventual defeat was done so at the hands of the metallic dragons who served Bahamut and a collection of traitors to the chromatic cause. Each dragon would be turned by the respective dragon soul of its natural enemy. In this case, Emni the Undimmed and Redvassyl the Ashen Guard is the reason why Volcanis' reign of fire ended, he gave over his physical form to the golden\nDragonborn\n. However the Abisahi, particularly Shim the Mouth, is interested in reinstating the Chromatic Dragons back to their original status as tyrant kings of this petal of existence in Tiamat's name.\nHis dragonrider is a fire genasi named Blaine Cowen, and his task is to protect the Cinder Seal.\n===\n---\ntype: lore\ntitle: \"Zepheeron The Thunderfury\"\nsource: [\"World Anvil\"]\nworld_anvil_category: \"\"\n---\n\n# Zepheeron The Thunderfury\n\nBronze Dragon who is in charge of the Apparatus, also spends time volunteering at\nthe Ice House\n, minting\nElectrum\n. Hunted by the Abisahi who now control\nVolcanis the Scorched Tyrant\n.\nOne of the only possible operators of the Apparatus, along with Temptus (Kelik or his father), also requires one of the elemental seals.","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"2ddfb8c1-20a5-4586-9e3f-56e835fb2d23","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:48.734Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fb520fd6-db80-46e5-895d-66c86c4b1e30","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"251a72ad-c0ff-489f-9350-9ff095b17568","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"b88eb14b-7f26-44e9-9eb0-5548f9f74212","agentId":"a6a165a11de3f31fe","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01N8vDkFpq4BPnmYb8DFKwRh","type":"tool_result","content":"---\ntype: lore\ntitle: \"Alexandria\"\nsource: [\"World Anvil\"]\nworld_anvil_category: \"\"\n---\n\n# Alexandria\n\nAlternative Name(s)\nEuclid's Study, Hall of Living Memory\nType\nPlanetoid / Moon\nOwner/Ruler\nEuclid\n===\n---\ntype: lore\ntitle: \"Balazar Keerin\"\nsource: [\"World Anvil\"]\nworld_anvil_category: \"\"\n---\n\n# Balazar Keerin\n\nThe Archbishop of Bahamut and only Platinum\nDragonborn\n. Serves on the\nCouncil of Curators\n. He rules from his seat within the Parthenon of Conquis Abbey from which he serves as a moderator between the Scales of Justice and the Scales of Righteousness. Balazar is effectively ageless and is charged with protecting the Heart of the Faith... which was recently stolen.\n===\n---\ntype: lore\ntitle: \"Booksworth Academy\"\nsource: [\"World Anvil\"]\nworld_anvil_category: \"\"\n---\n\n# Booksworth Academy\n\nAarakocra Academy for the Gifted. Includes a variety of multidisciplinary classes and majors.\nAlumni:\nSargent Professor Edwin Forebeak\nEugine Grakus\nFinneas Tux\nCurrent Headmistress: Mother Makaw\n===\n---\ntype: lore\ntitle: \"Council of Curators\"\nsource: [\"World Anvil\"]\nworld_anvil_category: \"\"\n---\n\n# Council of Curators\n\nGroup of the Five Leaders of the Dragonborn Tribes:\nThe Lit of the\nSummerborn\n-\nHelm Goldentooth\nThe Nic of the\n===\n---\ntype: lore\ntitle: \"Sand Seas\"\nsource: [\"World Anvil\"]\nworld_anvil_category: \"\"\n---\n\n# Sand Seas\n\nThe headquarters for the\nQual' Xar\nNation. Founded by the Third\nKraken of the Sand Seas\n, Oswald Mangacog, the whole city is rumored to behave like a mirage under his whim.\nThe Dunes serve as a field of portals with preprogrammed \"guides\" to teach newcomers the new rules while within the city. The first of which is that nowhere in the Sand Seas requires travel to find. Instead the citizen may visualize where they need to go and the sand crabs will deliver traveler there at incredible speeds!\n===\n---\ntype: lore\ntitle: \"Hydillia\"\nsource: [\"World Anvil\"]\nworld_anvil_category: \"\"\n---\n\n# Hydillia\n\nA Sprite of the Spring Court and former oath of the chain familiar with\nEvelyandrin of the Spring Court\nand her champion, Akoti.\nVery anxious and weary of the color red.\n===\n---\ntype: lore\ntitle: \"Jack the Beanstalk\"\nsource: [\"World Anvil\"]\nworld_anvil_category: \"\"\n---\n\n# Jack the Beanstalk\n\nThe Giant Beanstalk which anchors Cloud City together as well as to the greater landmass of Dawnhaven. Recently severed though the culprit is rumored. Some blame the arrival of Volcanis, other parties such as the Yuan-ti claim to be the mastermind behind the plot. The consequences of this break is one of the main reasons why the city is falling so quickly. This aligns with the parties known goals of both the orcs and fiends.\nPurpose / Function\nThere is a potion of creation inside Erlameyer's Flask that might be used to repair the Beanstalk, The Storm Giant Blacksmith, Thestrus also created a plant slaying sword called Jack's Bane which is said to be able to cut right through the beanstalk. Thestrus claimed that his sword was stolen and returned the night the king was killed. The orcs climbed up the beanstalk with the intention of fulfilling a prophecy recovered when they raided the Children Of Eavallan Forest and extracted their prophecy of Guard'Deamon.\n===\n---\ntype: lore\ntitle: \"Firekeeper Morn Loregramar\"\nsource: [\"World Anvil\"]\nworld_anvil_category: \"\"\n---\n\n# Firekeeper Morn Loregramar\n\nElder Orcish Shaman of the Earth Mother. Of the Red Hands of Faeish and credited with finding the Children of Eavallan Forest, sparking a war between the Elves within and the Orcish people. Husband of the leader of the Faeish Tribe, Commander Barberum Loregramar.\nHis religion has iconography that pertains to the Twelve Zodiacs. Long ago he met a wild human woman attempting to steal a meal from him who he named \"Wild Spirit of Fire.\" Young Brigit took that name to heart and adopted it as her last name, Wildfire.\nA kind spirit who is at odds with the raiding nature of the orcish culture. He seems to follow a way of life that is more akin to the Druids than the murder and pillaging that his people find themselves in. Recently recovered by the party as he was attempting to rescue\nThe Amazing Dr. Grackel\nfrom a brutal fate.\nHis favorite phrase is Lok'Tava which means \"The Earth Mother Provides.\" This phrase was the password for a druid's grove within the Vodram Feywilde belonging to a Tribe of Progenitors.","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"99b431da-c5cc-4a03-b21f-3d73b783db1d","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:11:59.977Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"251a72ad-c0ff-489f-9350-9ff095b17568","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}

{"parentUuid":"5a459a28-3bef-4094-ba32-c633c0af509a","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a6a165a11de3f31fe","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01Tu8Yz5uyEhF4CaLrNLVXRy","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01YRXM6ipPqyc1FBhvQazh9L","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault/MOC.md","content":"---\ntype: moc\ntitle: \"Mardov Campaign — Map of Content\"\n---\n\n# Mardov Campaign Wiki\n\n> The world of Mardov -- a 136-chapter D&D campaign spanning 5 acts, 24 player characters, hundreds of NPCs, and a continent's worth of locations. 665 vault pages and growing.\n\n---\n\n## Chapters (141 pages)\n\nThe campaign is structured in 5 acts with parallel party tracks starting in Act 3.\n\n### [[chapters/act-1/index|Act 1 — The Awakening]] (Chapters 0-15)\nThe party is recruited by the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]], discovers [[Mordred]]'s soul-harvesting, dies and is reborn with [[Sabine Ra]]'s soul fragments, and liberates [[Argos]].\n\nKey chapters:\n- [[Chapter 00 - The Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] -- Party origin\n- [[Chapter 02 - The Gnome Effn Way]] -- Party killed, Sabine's sacrifice\n- [[Chapter 09 - Never Trust a Hat]] -- First Prophecy\n- [[Chapter 13 - Viva La Revolution]] -- Argos civil war, Third Prophecy\n\n### [[chapters/act-2/index|Act 2 — The Voyage and the Underdark]] (Chapters 17-41)\nSea voyage to the [[Magister's Circle]], Zodiac revelation, Underdark descent, [[Forgesworn]] legacy.\n\nKey chapters:\n- [[Chapter 20 - The Magisters Circle]] -- Arrival at the Circle\n- [[Chapter 23 - The Zodiacs Revealed]] -- Zodiac spirits appear\n- [[Chapter 30 - Into the Underdark]] -- Peraxis Phyrexia revealed\n- [[Chapter 36 - Lego My Myconid]] -- First Forge, Nocturnus crowned\n- [[Chapter 41 - The Party Splits]] -- Dwarves/Tieflings diverge\n\n### [[chapters/act-3/index|Act 3 — The Zenith Wars]] (Chapters 42-87)\n45+ chapters of parallel play: **Dwarves** (Vag'Mahar, Mountain's Zenith) and **Tieflings/Distillery** (DawnHaven, Cloud City, Fae).\n\nKey chapters:\n- [[Chapter 60 - Cloud City Parachute]] -- Cloud City\n- [[Chapter 67 - Team Distillery Begins]] -- Tieflings renamed\n- [[Chapter 75 - Birt Solo Mission]] -- Birt solo\n- [[Chapter 87 - The Emerald Veil]] -- New tracks emerge\n\n### [[chapters/act-4/index|Act 4 — The Final War]] (Chapters 88-102)\nCraftkeepers and Distillery converge. The Good Guys Guild forms. Guard'Daemon approaches.\n\nKey chapters:\n- [[Chapter 088 - Act 4 Begins]] -- Craftkeepers established\n- [[Chapter 100 - The Centennial]] -- Centennial chapter\n- [[Chapter 101 - The Good Guys Guild]] -- Guild formed\n\n### [[chapters/act-5/index|Act 5 — The Ascension]] (Chapters 104-136)\nThe endgame: Anchor ascensions, divine conflicts, the final battle for Mardov.\n\nKey chapters:\n- All four Zeniths must be resolved, Vecna's ascension must be stopped, and the Guard'Daemon must be prevented. Note: Chapters 103 and 129/134 are absent from this act's file set.\n\n## Party Structure\n\n- **Reunited party**: After the Act 4 reunion, the party operates as one group again (with occasional splits for parallel objectives)\n- Key PCs: [[Brigit Wildfire]], [[Daina Fireforge]], [[Bergs]], [[Nocturnus Wayne]], [[Varis Laindon]], [[Sprocket Overspark]], [[Fen Darkeyes]]\n\n## Chapters\n\n| # | Title | Key Events |\n|---|-------|------------|\n| 104 | [[chapter-104]] | Death of Reginald Providence; Capital-H Heroes |\n| 136 | [[chapter-136]] | The Final Battle; Tiamat fight; Bergs ascends to Godhood |\n\n## Major Themes\n\n- **Anchor Ascension**: The party must ascend each of the four Anchors at their respective Zeniths\n  - [[Mountain's Zenith]] / [[Anchor of War]] -- Chapters 104-105 (Vag'Mahar, First Forge)\n  - [[Winter's Zenith]] / [[Anchor of Pestilence]] -- Chapters 119-121 (Bynar, Halls of Winter)\n  - [[Forest's Zenith]] / [[Anchor of Death]] -- Chapters 123-128 (Machu)\n  - [[Harvest's Zenith]] / [[Anchor of Famine]] -- Chapters 132-133\n- **Divine Conflicts**: Vecna, Asmodeus, Bahamut, and Tiamat all become active participants\n- **Bergs' Apotheosis**: Bergs ascends to godhood, opening a rift between Mardov and [[Vodram]]\n- **Promise Wayne**: Killing Tiamat/Ashara allows Honor to take final form as Promise Wayne\n- **Vecna's Endgame**: With knowledge from fallen gods, Vecna can bring Oblivion and subjugate beings; calls the dragons to [[Machu]]\n- **The Alabaster Seat Departs**: The Alabaster Seat leaves Mardov, leaving the door wide open\n- **Forgesworn Legacy**: All 13 Maghar revealed, their blessings unlocked at the First Forge\n\n## Player Notes Coverage\n\nAll chapters 104-136 have Brigit's player notes in [[sources/lore-extraction/]]. Notable alternate perspectives:\n- Chapter 111: Group Notes (multiple authors)\n- Chapter 115A: Rufus's Notes\n- Chapter 125: Borgot's Notes\n\n## Data Gaps\n\n- Chapters 122, 129, and 134 have no player notes or are missing from the file set\n- Most chapters have DM slide decks as PDFs that need extraction (marked with `<!-- PDF extraction requires pdftotext -->`)\n- Chapter 104 is the most complete, with full DM Lead-In slides and Forgesworn Maghar appendix\n- Chapter 136 has DM planning notes (Tiamat fight ending, Nocturnus vignette, Bergs apotheosis consequences)\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":5443,"cache_read_input_tokens":74242,"output_tokens":1800,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":5443},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CZ9fAcwV2upnT6ixSoEsu","type":"assistant","uuid":"d52f5e7b-3706-45e5-b83f-b04998011687","timestamp":"2026-03-18T01:14:36.197Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"1d3bac73-775a-49a7-b9c3-a49425b36aca","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"virtual-sparking-emerson"}
